## Analyzer Architecture Summary

kuberay is a Kubernetes component. deploying 1 workload(s) (kuberay-operator) with 1 container(s). watching 7 resource type(s). exposing 1 service(s) on ports\: 8080. connecting to external services\: grpc, requests. runtime dependencies\: Redis. with 366 operator config constants (11 image references, 27 env vars). exposing 4 Prometheus metrics. Python k8s client with 23 API call(s) and 1 CRD kind reference(s). integrates with Prometheus, management relationship via labels/annotations.
